    + The nearest EV fast charger is 4.2 mi away; in similar neighborhoods it's about 0.7 mi.
    - The nearest public school is 0.8 mi away; in similar neighborhoods it's about 0.4 mi. 
    + Summer heat here is +1.6°F against this county's urban-area average; similar neighborhoods are about -0.9°F against theirs.
